<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">نضر بن کثیر ابوسہل ازدی کہتے ہیں کہ عبداللہ بن طاؤس نے منیٰ میں مسجد خیف میں میرے پہلو میں نماز پڑھی، تو انہوں نے جب پہلا سجدہ کیا، اور سجدے سے اپنا سر اٹھایا تو اپنے دونوں ہاتھوں کو چہرے کے بالمقابل اٹھایا، مجھے یہ بات عجیب لگی تو میں نے وہیب بن خالد سے کہا کہ یہ ایسا کام کر رہے ہیں جو میں نے کبھی کسی کو کرتے نہیں دیکھا؟، تو وہیب نے ان سے کہا: آپ ایسا کام کرتے ہیں جسے ہم نے کسی کو کرتے نہیں دیکھا؟ اس پر عبداللہ بن طاؤس نے کہا: میں نے اپنے والد کو ایسا کرتے دیکھا ہے، اور میرے والد نے کہا: میں نے عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہم کو ایسا کرتے دیکھا ہے، اور عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہم نے کہا: میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو ایسا کرتے دیکھا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1146]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1146" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Nadr bin Kathir Abu Sahl Azdi kehte hain ki Abdullah bin Tawus ne Mina mein Masjid-e-Khayf mein mere pehlu mein namaz padhi, toh unhone jab pehla sajda kiya aur sajde se apna sar uthaya toh apne dono hathon ko chehre ke bilmuqabil uthaya. Mujhe yeh baat ajeeb lagi toh maine Wuhayb bin Khalid se kaha ki yeh aisa kaam kar rahe hain jo maine kabhi kisi ko karte nahi dekha? Toh Wuhayb ne unse kaha: Aap aisa kaam karte hain jise hum ne kisi ko karte nahi dekha? Is par Abdullah bin Tawus ne kaha: Main ne apne walid ko aisa karte dekha hai, aur mere walid ne kaha: Main ne Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhum) ko aisa karte dekha hai, aur Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhum) ne kaha: Main ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko aisa karte dekha hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1146]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1146" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1142

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1147)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، دُحَيْمٌ قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا مَرْوَانُ بْنُ مُعَاوِيَةَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الأَصَمِّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي يَزِيدُ بْنُ الأَصَمِّ، عَنْ مَيْمُونَةَ، قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا سَجَدَ خَوَّى بِيَدَيْهِ حَتَّى يُرَى وَضَحُ إِبْطَيْهِ مِنْ وَرَائِهِ وَإِذَا قَعَدَ اطْمَأَنَّ عَلَى فَخِذِهِ الْيُسْرَى ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Maimunah said: &quot;When the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) prostrated, he would hold his arms out to his sides, so that the whiteness of his armpits could be seen from behind. And when he sat he rested on his left thigh</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्-उल-मोमिनीन मैमूना (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब सजदा करते तोह अपने दोनों हाथ अपने पहलु से जुड़ा रखते यहाँ तक की आप के पीछे आप की बग़ल की सफेदी नज़र आती तोह अपनी बायें राण ज़मीन पर लगा कर बैठते.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین میمونہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جب سجدہ کرتے تو اپنے دونوں ہاتھ اپنے پہلو سے جدا رکھتے یہاں تک کہ آپ کے پیچھے آپ کے بغل کی سفیدی نظر آتی، اور جب بیٹھتے تو اپنی بائیں ران زمین پر لگا کر بیٹھتے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-ul-Momineen Maimoona (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab sajda karte toh apne dono haath apne pehlu se juda rakhte yahan tak ki aap ke peeche aap ki baghal ki safedi nazar aati, aur jab baithte toh apni bayein raan zameen par laga kar baithte.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1147]</p>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ أَبُو قُدَامَةَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي الْحَكَمُ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي لَيْلَى، عَنِ الْبَرَاءِ، قَالَ كَانَ صَلاَةُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رُكُوعُهُ وَسُجُودُهُ وَقِيَامُهُ بَعْدَ مَا يَرْفَعُ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ الرُّكُوعِ وَبَيْنَ السَّجْدَتَيْنِ قَرِيبًا مِنَ السَّوَاءِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1148" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The source narrator reporting Muhammad&#x27;s explicitly attributed speech or action; narrator framing remains part of the account. Bara explicitly describes the relative length of the Messenger&#x27;s prayer postures.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Al-Bara&#x27; said: &quot;In the prayer of the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), his bowing, his prostration, standing after he raised his head from bowing and (sitting) between the two prostrations, were almost the same</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1148]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1148" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1144

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1149)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الأَحْوَصِ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، وَعَلْقَمَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُكَبِّرُ فِي كُلِّ رَفْعٍ وَوَضْعٍ وَقِيَامٍ وَقُعُودٍ وَأَبُو بَكْرٍ وَعُمَرُ وَعُثْمَانُ رضى الله عنهم ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Abdullah said: &quot;The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) used to say the takbir every time he got up, went down, stood and sat. Abu Bakr, &#x27;Umar, and &#x27;Uthman (did likewise)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हर उठते, झुकते, खड़े होते और बैठते वक़्त अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, और अबू बक्र वा उमर और उस्मान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुम) भी ऐसा ही करते थे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ہر اٹھتے، جھکتے، کھڑے ہوتے اور بیٹھتے وقت اللہ اکبر کہتے، اور ابوبکر و عمر اور عثمان رضی اللہ عنہم بھی۔</p>

## Narration 1145

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1150)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رَافِعٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا حُجَيْنٌ، - وَهُوَ ابْنُ الْمُثَنَّى - قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، عَنْ عُقَيْلٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، يَقُولُ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا قَامَ إِلَى الصَّلاَةِ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَقُومُ ثُمَّ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَرْكَعُ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ حِينَ يَرْفَعُ صُلْبَهُ مِنَ الرَّكْعَةِ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ وَهُوَ قَائِمٌ ‏&quot;‏ رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَهْوِي سَاجِدًا ثُمَّ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَرْفَعُ رَأْسَهُ ثُمَّ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَسْجُدُ ثُمَّ يُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَرْفَعُ رَأْسَهُ ثُمَّ يَفْعَلُ ذَلِكَ فِي الصَّلاَةِ كُلِّهَا حَتَّى يَقْضِيَهَا وَيُكَبِّرُ حِينَ يَقُومُ مِنَ الثِّنْتَيْنِ بَعْدَ الْجُلُوسِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   1150 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah said: &quot;When the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) stood to pray, he said the takbir, when he (started), then he said the takbir when he bowed, then he said: &#x27;Sami Allahu liman hamidah (Allah hears the one who praises Him)&#x27;, when he stood up from bowing. Then he said when he was standing: &#x27;Rabbana lakal-hamd.&#x27; Then he said the takbir when he went down in prostration, then he said the takbir when he raised his head, and he did that throughout the entire prayer until he finished it, and he said the takbir when he stood up after the first two rak&#x27;ahs, after sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   1150 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैराह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब नमाज़ के लिए उठते तो खड़े होते वक़्त अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब रुकू करते तो अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब अपनी पीठ रुकू से उठाते तो समी अल्लाहु लिमन हमिदाह कहते, फिर खड़े हो कर कहते: रब्बना लकल हम्द, फिर जब सजदे के लिए झुकते तो अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब सजदे से सर उठाते तो अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब दूसरा सजदा करते तो अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब सजदे से सर उठाते तो अल्लाहु अकबर कहते। फिर पूरी नमाज़ में ऐसा ही करते यहाँ तक के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नमाज़ पूरी कर लेते, और जब दूसरी रकअत से बैठने के बाद उठते तो भी अल्लाहु अकबर कहते।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جب نماز کے لیے اٹھتے تو جس وقت کھڑے ہوتے «اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر جب رکوع کرتے تو«اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر جس وقت اپنی پیٹھ رکوع سے اٹھاتے تو «سمع اللہ لمن حمده» کہتے، پھر کھڑے ہو کر کہتے: «ربنا لك الحمد» پھر جب سجدہ کے لیے جھکتے تو «اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر جب سجدہ سے سر اٹھاتے تو «اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر جب ( دوسرا ) سجدہ کرتے تو «اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر جب سجدہ سے سر اٹھاتے تو «اللہ اکبر» کہتے، پھر پوری نماز میں ایسا ہی کرتے یہاں تک کہ آپ اسے پوری کر لیتے، اور جس وقت دوسری رکعت سے بیٹھنے کے بعد اٹھتے تو ( بھی ) «اللہ اکبر» کہتے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab namaz ke liye uthte to khade hote waqt Allahu Akbar kehte, phir jab ruku karte to Allahu Akbar kehte, phir jab apni peeth ruku se uthate to Sami Allahu Liman Hamidah kehte, phir khade ho kar kehte: Rabbana Lakal Hamd, phir jab sajde ke liye jhukte to Allahu Akbar kehte, phir jab sajde se sar uthate to Allahu Akbar kehte, phir jab dusra sajda karte to Allahu Akbar kehte, phir jab sajde se sar uthate to Allahu Akbar kehte. Phir puri namaz mein aisa hi karte yahan tak ke aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) namaz puri kar lete, aur jab dusri rakat se baithne ke baad uthte to bhi Allahu Akbar kehte.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1150]</p>

## Narration 1146

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1151)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا زِيَادُ بْنُ أَيُّوبَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَيُّوبُ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلاَبَةَ، قَالَ جَاءَنَا أَبُو سُلَيْمَانَ مَالِكُ بْنُ الْحُوَيْرِثِ إِلَى مَسْجِدِنَا فَقَالَ أُرِيدُ أَنْ أُرِيَكُمْ كَيْفَ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُصَلِّي ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقَعَدَ فِي الرَّكْعَةِ الأُولَى حِينَ رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ السَّجْدَةِ الآخِرَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1151]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Abi Qibalah said: &quot;Abu Sulaiman Malik bin Al-Huwairith came to our masjid and said: &quot;I want to show you how I saw the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) pray.&#x27;&quot; He said: &quot;He sat during the first Rak&#x27;ah when he raised his head from the second prostration</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1151]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबु किलबह कहते हैं के अबु सुलईमान मालिक बिन हुवैरिथ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) हमारी मस्जिद में आये और कहने लगे: मैं तुम्हे दिखाना चाहता हूँ के मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को कैसे नमाज़ पढ़ते देखा है वह पहली रकात में आखरि सजदे से अपना सर उठाते वक़्त बैठे (जलसा-इ-इसतरहाट किया).</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1151]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوقلابہ کہتے ہیں کہ ابوسلیمان مالک بن حویرث رضی اللہ عنہ ہماری مسجد میں آئے اور کہنے لگے: میں تمہیں دکھانا چاہتا ہوں کہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو کیسے نماز پڑھتے دیکھا ہے، تو وہ پہلی رکعت میں آخری سجدہ سے اپنا سر اٹھاتے وقت بیٹھے ( جلسہ استراحت کرتے ) ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1151]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Qilabah kehte hain ke Abu Sulaiman Malik bin Huwairith (Radi Allahu Anhu) hamari masjid mein aaye aur kehne lage: Main tumhe dikhana chahta hoon ke maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko kaise namaz padhte dekha hai, to woh pehli rakat mein aakhri sajde se apna sar uthate waqt baithe (jalsa-e-istarahat kiya).</p>

## Narration 1147

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1152)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا عَلِيُّ بْنُ حُجْرٍ، قَالَ أَنْبَأَنَا هُشَيْمٌ، عَنْ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلاَبَةَ، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ، قَالَ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُصَلِّي فَإِذَا كَانَ فِي وَتْرٍ مِنْ صَلاَتِهِ لَمْ يَنْهَضْ حَتَّى يَسْتَوِيَ جَالِسًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1152]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1152" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Malik bin Al-Huwairith said: &quot;I saw the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) pray, and when he was in an odd-numbered rak&#x27;ah, he did not get up until he had settled in a sitting position</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1152]</p>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन हुवैरिस (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को नमाज़ पढ़ते देखा, जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अपनी नमाज़ की ताक रकअतों में होते तो जब तक बैठ कर सीधे नहीं हो जाते उठते नहीं थे।</p>

## Narration 1148

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1153)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا خَالِدٌ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلاَبَةَ، قَالَ كَانَ مَالِكُ بْنُ الْحُوَيْرِثِ يَأْتِينَا فَيَقُولُ أَلاَ أُحَدِّثُكُمْ عَنْ صَلاَةِ، رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَيُصَلِّي فِي غَيْرِ وَقْتِ الصَّلاَةِ فَإِذَا رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ السَّجْدَةِ الثَّانِيَةِ فِي أَوَّلِ الرَّكْعَةِ اسْتَوَى قَاعِدًا ثُمَّ قَامَ فَاعْتَمَدَ عَلَى الأَرْضِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1153" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Malik bin Al-Huwairith demonstrating what he explicitly identifies as the Messenger&#x27;s prayer. The source expressly frames Malik&#x27;s action as a demonstration of the Messenger&#x27;s prayer.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Abu Qibalah said: &quot;Malik bin Al-Huwairith used to come to us and say: &quot;Shall I not tell you about the prayer of the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)?&quot; He was praying at a time other than the time of prayer, and when he raised his head from the second prostration in the first rak&#x27;ah, he settled in a seated position, then he stood up, and he supported himself on the ground (while doing so)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1153]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1153" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू किलाबा कहते हैं के मलिक बिन हुवैरिथ (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) हमारे पास आते तो कहते: क्या मैं तुम्हे रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की नमाज़ के बारे में न बताऊं? वो नमाज़ पढ़ते, जब वो पहली रकात में दूसरे सजदे से अपना सर उठाते तो बैठ कर सीधे हो जाते, फिर खड़े होते तो ज़मीन पर अपना हाथ टेकते।</p>

## Narration 1149

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1154)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ مَنْصُورٍ، قَالَ أَنْبَأَنَا يَزِيدُ بْنُ هَارُونَ، قَالَ أَنْبَأَنَا شَرِيكٌ، عَنْ عَاصِمِ بْنِ كُلَيْبٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ وَائِلِ بْنِ حُجْرٍ، قَالَ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا سَجَدَ وَضَعَ رُكْبَتَيْهِ قَبْلَ يَدَيْهِ وَإِذَا نَهَضَ رَفَعَ يَدَيْهِ قَبْلَ رُكْبَتَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ لَمْ يَقُلْ هَذَا عَنْ شَرِيكٍ غَيْرُ يَزِيدَ بْنِ هَارُونَ وَاللَّهُ تَعَالَى أَعْلَمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Wa&#x27;il bin Hujr said:&#x27;I saw the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) when he prostrated, place his knees on the ground before his hands, and when he got up, he lifted his hands before his knees</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">वाईल बिन हुज्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को देखा के जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) सजदा करते तो घुटनों को हाथ से पहले जमीन पर रखते घुटनो से पहले उठाते. अबु अब्दुर रहमान (नासा&#x27;ई) कहते हैं: इसे शरीक़ से यज़ीद बिन हारुन के इलावा किसी और ने रिवायत नहीं किया है</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وائل بن حجر رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو دیکھا کہ جب آپ سجدہ کرتے تو گھٹنوں کو ہاتھ سے پہلے زمین پر رکھتے، اور جب اٹھتے تو اپنے ہاتھوں کو گھٹنوں سے پہلے اٹھاتے۔ ابوعبدالرحمٰن ( نسائی ) کہتے ہیں: اسے شریک سے یزید بن ہارون کے علاوہ کسی اور نے روایت نہیں کیا ہے، واللہ تعالیٰ اعلم۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Wail bin Hujr (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko dekha ke jab aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) sajda karte to ghutno ko hath se pehle zameen par rakhte, aur jab uthte to apne hathon ko ghutno se pehle uthate. Abu Abdur Rahman (Nasa&#x27;i) kehte hain: Ise Shareek se Yazeed bin Haroon ke ilawa kisi aur ne riwayat nahi kiya hai, Wallahu A&#x27;lam.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1154]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، أَنَّ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، كَانَ يُصَلِّي بِهِمْ فَيُكَبِّرُ كُلَّمَا خَفَضَ وَرَفَعَ فَإِذَا انْصَرَفَ قَالَ وَاللَّهِ إِنِّي لأَشْبَهُكُمْ صَلاَةً بِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1155" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Hurairah demonstrating his prayer and giving his own comparison with the Messenger&#x27;s. The supplied actions and comparative claim retain Abu Hurairah as their actual actor and speaker.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Abu Salamah :That Abu Hurairah used to lead them in prayer, and he said the takbir when he went down and came up. When he had finished he said: &#x27;By Allah (SWT), I am the one among you whose prayer most closely resembles that of the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1155]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1155" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू सलामा कहते हैं के अबू हुरैरा (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) उन्हें नमाज़ पढ़ाते थे, तो जब जब झुकते और उठते अल्लाहु अकबर कहते, फिर जब वो सलाम फेर कर पलटते तो कहते: क़सम अल्लाह की, मैं तुम में नमाज़ के एतबार से रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सब से ज़्यादा मुशाबिहत रखता हूं।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1155" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>
